> <!-- COMMENTARY:
>    FIDELITY MAP.
>    Simile: griffin (L1); gold/theft (L1,3,4); wilderness + hill or marshy dale (L2,
>    "hill and hollow" = the wild high/low terrain, wilderness subsumed in it); winged
>    course (L2 "hunting wings"); pursues (L1 "gives chase", L3 "hounds"); Arimaspians
>    (L3, named, m-stress unstaved); by stealth had stolen the guarded gold (L4).
>    Fiend: so eagerly (L5 "fevered"); pursues his way (L5 "forces its way"); terrain
>    catalog bog/steep/strait/rough/dense/rare—all six kept (L6-7); body catalog
>    head/hands/wings/feet -> head/hand/heel/wing, all four (L8); verb-pentad
>    swims/sinks/wades/creeps/flies verbatim (L9).
>    Uproar: at length (L10 "then"); universal uproar wild (L10 "din... unbound");
>    stunning sounds (L10 din + L12 the stunning hammer-impact); voices all confused
>    (L11 "voices unvoiced"); born through the hollow dark (L10 "from the dark"; "hollow"
>    dropped); assaults his ear with loudest vehemence (L12 "hammers his hearing with
>    hugest force").
>    Resolve: thither he plies, undaunted (L13); to meet whatever power or spirit of the
>    nethermost abyss might dwell in that noise (L14 "what fathers in the pit"—"power
>    or spirit" compressed to the abyss-spawn, "spirit" folded in); to ask which way the
>    nearest coast of darkness lies, bordering on light (L15; "nearest" dropped).
>    Court: throne of Chaos + pavilion spread wide on the wasteful deep (L16-17); with
>    him enthroned sable-vested Night, eldest of things, consort of his reign (L18
>    "sable Night, naught named before her"—"consort/enthroned" implied by her station
>    beside Chaos); Orcus & Hades (L19); dreaded name of Demogorgon (L20); Rumor, Chance
>    (L21); Tumult, Confusion, all embroiled (L22); Discord with a thousand various
>    mouths (L23). Compressions ("spirit", "hollow", "nearest", "consort"/"enthroned")
>    noted above; the only amplifications are the personifying epithets on figures Milton
>    merely names (ashen Hades, grim Demogorgon, ruthless Chance)—justified in the
>    ledger, no padding, 1.0x length.
>
>    KENNING LEDGER (my coinages/epithets; each justified in one clause; phrase verbatim + line):
>    - "hunting wings" (L2): the griffin's winged pursuit-course.
>    - "stored-up hoard" (L4): the guarded gold—carries opposition stored<->stolen.
>    - "din drums" (L10): noise made an actor—agency-inversion.
>    - "voices unvoiced" (L11): voices drowned to noise—privation ("all confused").
>    - "what fathers in the pit" (L14): the abyss-spawn (power/spirit)—opposition face<->fathers.
>    - "court wide-flung" (L16): the dark pavilion spread wide.
>    - "wallowing deep" (L17): the wasteful deep set churning—agency-inversion.
>    - "naught named before her" (L18): Night as eldest of things—privation Night<->naught.
>    - "ashen Hades" (L19): the death-god's pallor; supplies the Orcus<->ashen vowel-stave.
>    - "grim Demogorgon, guessed by none" (L20): the dread, unknowable name—privation.
>    - "Rumor runs" (L21): rumor as runaway actor—agency-inversion.
>    - "ruthless Chance" (L21): Chance's pitiless caprice.
>    - "Discord throngs a thousand throats" (L23): strife crowding a thousand voices
>      ("a thousand various mouths")—agency-inversion.
>
>    VERIFICATION (both directions): each ledger phrase above appears verbatim at its
>    cited line; the FORM/SCANSION examples (staved-final L15/L18/L23, 5-lift L9, the
>    cluster cases) quote only words present in the poem. No orphaned ledger entries;
>    no FORM example quoting absent words. -->
>
> <div class="poem">
> Its gold gone, the griffin gives chase, <!-- 2+2: GOLD GONE || GRIF-fin chase [g: aa|ax] {privation: griffin ↔ gold(gone)} --> \
> over hill and hollow, on hunting wings, <!-- 2+2: HILL HOL-low || HUNT-ing wings [h: aa|ax] {opposition: hill ↔ hollow} --> \
> it hounds the Arimaspians, who hold its prize— <!-- 2+2: HOUNDS a-ri-MAS-pians || HOLD prize [h: ax|ax] {opposition: hounds ↔ hold}—Arimaspians (m-stress) sits unstaved per graceful-degradation for the proper name --> \
> by stealth they had stolen the stored-up hoard. <!-- 2+2: STEALTH STO-len || STORED hoard [st: aa|ax] {opposition: stored ↔ stolen} --> \
>
> So the fiend, fevered, forces its way, <!-- 2+2: FIEND FE-vered || FOR-ces way [f: aa|ax] {causation: fevered ↔ forces} --> \
> it bulls through bog, breasts the steep, <!-- 2+2: BULLS BOG || BREASTS steep [b: aa|ax] {opposition: bulls ↔ bog—the fiend's force vs the clinging mire} --> \
> through dense and rare, through rough and strait, <!-- 2+2: DENSE RARE || ROUGH STRAIT [r: ax|ax] {none—terrain catalog; r binds rare ↔ rough enumeratively; kept for fidelity to Milton's six-term terrain list} --> \
> with head and hand, on heel and wing, <!-- 2+2: HEAD HAND || HEEL WING [h: aa|ax] {none—body-part catalog; enumerative, kept for fidelity} --> \
> it swims or sinks, or wades, or creeps, or flies. <!-- 5 lifts (2+3): SWIMS SINKS || WADES CREEPS FLIES [s: aa | x x x] {opposition: swims ↔ sinks}—GUARDRAIL: Milton's verb-pentad preserved verbatim; meter (5 lifts) and cross-caesura stave deliberately sacrificed per graceful-degradation; only swims ↔ sinks carry a stave --> \
>
> Then din drums from the dark, unbound— <!-- 2+2: DIN DRUMS || DARK un-BOUND [d: aa|ax] {inversion: din ↔ drums—the noise made the actor} --> \
> voices unvoiced, in a vast blur, <!-- 2+2: VOI-ces un-VOICED || VAST blur [v: aa|ax] {privation: voices ↔ unvoiced—voices stripped of voice to noise} --> \
> it hammers his hearing with hugest force— <!-- 2+2: HAM-mers HEAR-ing || HU-gest force [h: aa|ax] {violence: hammers ↔ hearing—force-verb on its victim} --> \
>
> undaunted, he drives toward the din's heart, <!-- 2+2: un-DAUN-ted DRIVES || DIN'S heart [d: aa|ax] {opposition: undaunted ↔ din—the fearless will vs the fearful noise} --> \
> bold to face what fathers in the pit, <!-- 2+2: bold FACE || FA-thers pit [f: ax|ax] {opposition: face ↔ fathers—the fiend confronts what the abyss begets} --> \
> to ask where dark from dawn divides— <!-- 2+2: ask DARK || DAWN di-VIDES [d: xa|aa] {opposition: dark ↔ dawn—the border of darkness and light}—GUARDRAIL: staved final lift (divides), accepted to set the dark/dawn opposition and its dividing verb on one stave --> \
>
> lo—Chaos crowned, his court wide-flung, <!-- 2+2: CHA-os CROWNED || COURT wide-flung [k: aa|ax] {inversion: Chaos ↔ crowned—the formless made a crowned king} --> \
> wide on the waste of the wallowing deep, <!-- 2+2: WIDE WASTE || WAL-low-ing deep [w: aa|ax] {inversion: waste ↔ wallowing—the void granted churning motion}—GUARDRAIL: dense w-trio (wide/waste/wallowing) justified by the deep's chaotic surge, the inversion carrying the line --> \
> and sable Night, naught named before her— <!-- 2+2: SA-ble NIGHT || NAUGHT NAMED [n: xa|aa] {privation: Night ↔ naught—naught named before her: the eldest of things}—GUARDRAIL: staved final lift (named); "sable" (s) carried from Milton, unstaved --> \
> by them stand Orcus and ashen Hades, <!-- 2+2: stand OR-cus || ASH-en Ha-des [V: ax|ax] {none—court roster; vowel binds Orcus ↔ ashen (the Orcus/awe/ash license); naming set-piece} --> \
> and grim Demogorgon, guessed by none, <!-- 2+2: GRIM de-mo-GOR-gon || GUESSED none [g: aa|ax] {privation: Demogorgon ↔ guessed—the dread name no one can know}—"grim" is the one permitted mood-word --> \
> then Rumor runs, and ruthless Chance, <!-- 2+2: RU-mor RUNS || RUTH-less Chance [r: aa|ax] {inversion: Rumor ↔ runs—the abstraction made to run}—"ruthless" is the one permitted mood-word for Chance --> \
> Tumult and Confusion, tangled, at war, <!-- 2+2: TU-mult con-FU-sion || TAN-gled war [t: ax|ax] {like-on-like: Tumult ↔ tangled—the discord-powers embroiled, each in each} --> \
> and Discord throngs a thousand throats. <!-- 2+2: DIS-cord THRONGS || THOU-sand THROATS [th: xa|aa] {inversion: Discord ↔ throngs—strife made to crowd a thousand throats}—GUARDRAIL: staved final lift (throats); th-trio justified by "a thousand various mouths" -->
> </div>

## Claude-5-Sonnet

2026-06-30, max thinking:

> <!-- FORM
> Four stressed lifts per line (2+2), split by a marked caesura ( || ).
> Preferred pattern aa|ax: both lifts of half-line 1 alliterate, plus the first lift of half-line 2; the final lift is free. Achieved in 24/26 lines.
> One deliberate aa|xx (line 7): a low-ornament catalog line, tension withheld on purpose.
> One deliberate cross-caesura stave-switch (line 26): repetition-device (X against X | Y against Y), sanctioned by the brief's own "Sound against sound, voice into voice broken" exemplar.
> Vowel-alliteration license (all vowel-initial stressed syllables form one class) used for Orcus and Arimaspians.
> Anacrusis (unstressed lead-in before the first lift) at two openings (12, 21) to admit an initial modifier or a long name without inflating the lift-count.
> No Old English lexis, no faux-archaism; "o'er" not used.
> -->
>
> <!-- ARGUMENT ARC
> VP1 (1-4): the simile proper—a griffin, robbed of its gold, hunts the Arimaspian thieves; guard loses to guile. Pivots into the outcast's own flight.
> VP2 (5-8): the crossing—terrain and body catalog, undifferentiated struggle through Chaos's medium.
> VP3 (9-11): the uproar—noise as assailant, escalating from one dark howl to a sourceless, senseless totality.
> VP4 (12-14): the resolve—undaunted, he presses toward the noise, seeking the seam between dark and dawn.
> VP5 (15-18): the throne—Chaos enthroned in his own disorder's negation; Night beside him, defined by the stars she precedes.
> VP6 (19-26): the court—the eight named figures in Milton's order, closing on Discord, whose multiplying voice cancels itself.
> -->
>
> <!-- SCANSION KEY
> Stress-casing: CAPS marks the lifted syllable in audit comments.
> Stave notation: lowercase letter = alliterating consonant; "v" = vowel-class (all vowel-initial stressed syllables alliterate with one another). [x: aa|ax] names the achieved pattern.
> Tension vocabulary: opposition | privation | causation | like-on-like | inversion, tagged {tag: word↔word}.
> Pronunciation commitments—Arimaspians: AR-i-mas-pi-ans, stressed initially for vowel-alliteration (natural lexical stress falls on -MAS-; overridden here, per classical-name convention). Demogorgon: de-mo-GOR-gon, stress on GOR, alliterates hard-G. Orcus: OR-cus, stress initial, vowel-class. Chaos: KAY-os, hard-C/K, a distinct stave from the "ch" /tʃ/ of Chance.
> -->
>
> <!-- COMMENTARY
> Fidelity map. Milton's two lines of pure scene-setting (wilderness, hill, marshy dale) are treated as one substantive element and compressed into "ground-hunting," freeing room for the guard/guile opposition that is the simile's actual argument. The terrain catalog (bog/steep/strait/rough/dense/rare) is redistributed across two lines using alliterating near-synonym pairs—bog/bluff for low/high, thick/thinned for Milton's own built-in dense/rare antonym pair. The verb catalog (swim/sink/wade/creep/fly) compresses from five items to four (swim/sink/slog/fly); "slog" absorbs wade and creep's shared sense of laborious ground-motion—the catalog *effect* survives at full force with one fewer lexical item, which is the trade I judged worth making. "Power or spirit... of the nethermost abyss... might dwell in that noise"—Milton's amplificatory doublet—collapses into personified "Murk"; preserving both nouns cost more in line-economy than it returned in fidelity. The Night couplet (17-18) draws directly on this brief's own worked exemplar ("Sable-swathed Night, star-shorn, first-born"), already a correct 2+2 rendering of "sable-vested Night, eldest of things"—I could not improve on "star-shorn" as a privation-epithet for a goddess who predates the stars, so I did not try. "The dreaded name" is reattached specifically to Demogorgon (line 21), matching Milton's own syntax, where the phrase modifies Demogorgon and not Orcus or Hades. "A thousand various mouths" survives as "throng of thousand throats" in the naming-line, banking "mouth" to return with full force in the closing repetition.
>
> Kenning ledger. gold-robbed (griffin): privation, ties the guardian to the gold it no longer holds. ground-hunting (griffin): pure economy, absorbs wilderness/hill/dale into one motion-epithet. oath-breakers' (Orcus's epithet): causation, names what Orcus's dread produces, so a reader who doesn't know the myth isn't lost. sable-swathed / star-shorn / first-born (Night): adapted from the brief's own exemplar, not independently coined. throng of thousand throats (Discord): periphrasis rather than a true compound; chosen to keep Milton's numeral while banking "mouth" for the last line.
> -->
>
> <div class="poem">
> As a griffin, gold-robbed, || goes ground-hunting, <!-- 2+2: GRIFfin GOLD-robbed | GROUND HUNTing [g: aa|ax] {privation: griffin↔gold} --> \
> eyeing the Arimaspians—|| art in thieving— <!-- 2+2: EYEing ARimaspians | ART thieVING [v: aa|ax] {opposition: eyeing(vigilance)↔art(cunning); GUARDRAIL: Arimaspians stressed initially, non-natural, for vowel-alliteration} --> \
> who by guile got || guarded gold, now vanished. <!-- 2+2: GUILE GOT | GUARDed VANished [g: aa|ax] {opposition: guard↔guile} --> \
> So, eager as onset, || the outcast flew, <!-- 2+2: EAger ONset | OUTcast FLEW [v: aa|ax] {privation: outcast↔cast-out state} --> \
>
> over bog and bluff, || through broken country, <!-- 2+2: BOG BLUFF | BROken COUNtry [b: aa|ax] {opposition: bog(low)↔bluff(high)} --> \
> through thickets thick, || or thinned to bareness, <!-- 2+2: THICKets THICK | THINNED BAREness [th: aa|ax] {opposition: thick↔thinned} --> \
> with head or hand, || with wing or foot, <!-- 2+2: HEAD HAND | WING FOOT [h: aa|xx] {none—Milton's body-part catalog kept plain; tension deliberately withheld so the following verb-line carries full force} --> \
> he swims, or sinks, || or slogs, or flies. <!-- 2+2: SWIMS SINKS | SLOGS FLIES [s: aa|ax] {opposition: swims(float)↔sinks(descend); like-on-like: swims/sinks/slogs enact futile struggle} --> \
>
> At last his hearing, hurt || by a howling dark, <!-- 2+2: HEARing HURT | HOWLing DARK [h: aa|ax] {inversion: dark/howl(agent)↔hearing(patient)} --> \
> a clamor confused, || a crash of voices, <!-- 2+2: CLAmor conFUSED | CRASH VOICes [c: aa|ax] {privation: clamor(sound)↔confused(sense lacking)} --> \
> sound, sourceless, || senseless, spread wide— <!-- 2+2: SOUND SOURCEless | SENSEless WIDE [s: aa|ax] {privation: sound↔sourceless/senseless} --> \
>
> Undaunted, he meant || to meet what Murk might hide, <!-- 2+2: MEANT MEET | MURK HIDE [m: aa|ax] {opposition: meet(confrontation)↔hide(concealment); GUARDRAIL: anacrusis, "Undaunted, he" unstressed lead-in before the 2+2 count} --> \
> wondering which way || would win him passage <!-- 2+2: WONdering WAY | WOULD PASsage [w: aa|ax] {privation: wondering(not-knowing)↔way(the unknown route)} --> \
> to where murk || meets morning's edge. <!-- 2+2: MURK MEETS | MORning's EDGE [m: aa|ax] {opposition: murk(dark)↔morning(light), border enacted by "meets"} --> \
>
> There Chaos claimed || a chair of rule, <!-- 2+2: CHAos CLAIMED | CHAIR RULE [k: aa|ax] {opposition: Chaos(disorder)↔chair(order's throne)} --> \
> his drapery dark, || drawn over waste, <!-- 2+2: DRApery DARK | DRAWN WASTE [d: aa|ax] {causation: drawn(craft)↔waste(raw material it covers)} --> \
> Sable-swathed Night, || star-shorn, first-born, <!-- 2+2: SAble-swathed NIGHT | STAR-shorn first-BORN [s: aa|ax] {privation: Night↔stars} --> \
> his consort, crowned, || queen, unequaled— <!-- 2+2: CONsort CROWNED | QUEEN unEQualed [k: aa|ax] {privation: consort(secondary status)↔crowned(elevation)} --> \
>
> Orcus, oath-breakers' || awe, and their downfall, <!-- 2+2: ORcus OATH-breakers' | AWE DOWNfall [v: aa|ax] {causation: Orcus(agent)↔awe(the dread he produces)} --> \
> and Hades, holding || hordes past returning, <!-- 2+2: HAdes HOLDing | HORDES reTURNing [h: aa|ax] {causation: Hades(agent)↔hordes(what he holds)} --> \
> and grim Demogorgon || gagged, his name unspoken— <!-- 2+2: GRIM demoGORgon | GAGGED unSPOken [g: aa|ax] {privation: Demogorgon(name)↔gagged(enforced silence)} --> \
> Rumor went roving, || rootless, unresting— <!-- 2+2: RUmor ROVing | ROOTless unRESting [r: aa|ax] {privation: rumor↔rootless} --> \
> and Chance, no choice at all—|| chained to nothing, <!-- 2+2: CHANCE CHOICE | CHAINED NOThing [ch: aa|ax] {opposition: chance(randomness)↔choice(design)} --> \
> Tumult, tangled || with torn Confusion, <!-- 2+2: TUmult TANgled | TORN conFUsion [t: aa|ax] {like-on-like: tumult/tangled/torn enact mutual entanglement} --> \
> Then, a throng of thousand || throats: Discord. <!-- 2+2: THRONG THOUsand | THROATS DIScord [th: aa|ax] {privation: Discord↔excluded from the stave-concord itself—Discord is the free lift, formally enacting her nature} --> \
> voice against voice, || mouth against mouth. <!-- 2+2: VOICE against VOICE | MOUTH against MOUTH [v-v|m-m, stave-switch] {like-on-like: self-multiplying voice cancels into noise; GUARDRAIL: deliberate cross-caesura stave-switch, sanctioned by the brief's own "Sound against sound, voice into voice broken" exemplar} --> \
> </div>

## GPT-6 Astra

[GPT-6 Astra](https://openai.com/index/gpt-6-astra/), Ultra (2026-09-06):

> <!-- FORM Length: 28 verse lines against 23 source lines, 1.217x; below the 29-line integral cap. Meter: 2+2 primary lifts in L02-L28 under the declared reading; L01 deliberately has 2+3. Preference: aa|ax; xa|ax and ax|ax occur; patterns with a missing head-stave remain explicit FAILs. Cluster rule: adopted; st, sp, sk/sc match only themselves; sh and th are separate from s and t. Other clusters: ordinary initial consonant governs; cl and cr may bind on k, as in L12. Vowels: all stressed vowel onsets may bind, including OR-cus; spelling before an internal stress does not count. L01: "gives way to stealth" retains three lifts to state the collapse of guarding without recasting stealth as physical injury. L09: "freed from their footholds" participates in aa|aa; the fourth lift also alliterates. L14: "its utmost onslaught" participates in aa|aa; the fourth lift also alliterates. L02-L04, L06-L08, L10-L11, L16-L18, L21-L28: deliberate formal or semantic FAILs, individually justified below. L01 also FAILs the four-lift requirement, although its semantic tension passes. No planned or detected end-rhyme; no Old English vocabulary or archaic inflection. No compound-noun catalog and no quarantined phrasing imported into the hybrid. -->
> <!-- ARGUMENT ARC L01-L04: the defeated watch and pursuing griffin become the measure of the fiend's eagerness. L05-L09: hostile terrain, the entire bodily catalog, and the five modes of movement. L10-L14: universal, stunning, confused noise travels through hollow darkness and assaults the ear. L15-L19: undaunted approach, the possible abyssal inhabitant, and the request for darkness's nearest light-facing coast. L20-L24a: the revelation of Chaos's court, pavilion, and Night's shared sovereignty and precedence. L24b-L28: the ordered attendants, the dreaded name, collective embroilment, and Discord's thousand various mouths. -->
> <!-- SCANSION KEY L01-L28 number verse lines, ignoring blank lines and comments. CAPS mark the stressed syllable of each primary lift, not a whole word's typographic initial. 2+2 states the intended primary lifts; unstressed grammar and subordinate secondary accents are omitted. The spaced double-pipe separates verse half-lines and their scans; single pipes occur only inside bracketed pattern notation. [g: aa|ax] names the shared onset and lift pattern; a staves, x does not; V means the common vowel license. [—: xx|xx] means no cross-caesural stave, even if a half-line alliterates internally. Tags: opposition | privation | causation | like-on-like | inversion | violence; {none} refuses a semantic claim. PASS requires both the substantive binding and the stated form; PASS-tension does not cancel FAIL-meter. Internal stresses: ari-MAS-pi-ans; a-BYSS; e-RUPTS; pa-VIL-ion; demo-GOR-gon; con-FU-sion; to-GETH-er. Names: CHA-os = KAY-oss; OR-cus = OR-kuss; HA-des = HAY-deez; RU-mor = ROO-mer; TU-mult = TOO-mult; DIS-cord = DIS-kord. Ordinary primary stresses: UP-roar, ON-slaught, EN-ter-ing, CLAR-i-ty, VAR-i-ous; DAY-light has one primary lift here. No proper name gains an invented initial lift to manufacture a stave; secondary syllabic stresses remain subordinate. At L18, lies is subordinate: ASK COAST / CLOSE-st HAND; at L22, with him is an unstressed tail. -->
> <!-- COMMENTARY Fidelity map (source S01-S23; hybrid L01-L28): S01-S04 -> L01-L04: griffin, wilderness, winged travel, hills, marshy valleys, Arimaspians, stealth, guarded gold, pursuit, and eager fiend all remain. The theft is placed before pursuit to expose its causal force; this restores the event order already implied by the source's past perfect. L01's watch giving way is an amplification of failed guarding, not an injury to a guard; L02's hauled off makes the stolen gold the cause of pursuit. L03's hollows of marsh compresses marshy dale; hill/hollow opposition is real, but the verb rule still fails. S05 -> L05-L06: bog, steep, strait, rough, dense, rare -> bogs, broken climbs, strait, rough, dense, thin. L05 amplifies difficult traversal into battling terrain; broken applies the source's roughness to its climbs and makes them resistance, not an added battle or a new geographical episode. S06 -> L07: head, hands, wings, feet remain complete and in order. S07 -> L08-L09: swims, sinks, wades, creeps, flies remain complete and in order; none is weakened into a generic moves. L09 amplifies flight into release of the feet from their footholds; the body relinquishes supporting terrain, without an added fall, injury, or failed step. S08-S11a -> L10-L14: eventual arrival, universal uproar, sounds, voices, stunning confusion, hollow darkness, assault on the ear, and maximal force all remain. L12 amplifies confused voices into loss of intelligibility; clamor destroys clarity rather than merely sharing its mood. L13, imported from B12 with syntax integrated, gives the source's carrying medium agency; no new carrier or event is added. L14's utmost onslaught renders loudest vehemence; entering carries the source's assault into the ear and claims no permanent deafness. S11b-S13 -> L15-L17: approach toward the noise, undaunted resolve, an unspecified spirit or power, the lowest abyss, and residence in the noise all remain. L15 turns undaunted into a negated restraint: fear does not stop approach; this does not claim that he cannot feel fear. Power/spirit reverse locally at L16 for syntax; neither is omitted. Lower-case tumult in L17 means noise, not the later named attendant. S14-S15a -> L18-L19: the question retains the nearest coast, not the easiest journey; daylight bounds darkness. L19's dammed translates a coast into a limiting metaphor; it adds no masonry, current, retreat, or victory of light over dark. S15b-S17 -> L20-L22: the throne of Chaos, the dark pavilion, its breadth, and the wasteful deep remain. L20's court makes the existing throne, joint reign, and attendants explicit; the tension is disorder maintaining an institution of rule, not Chaos merely doing a verb. Wasteful deep becomes waste plus deep across L21-L22; spread and wide are both retained. The pavilion clause is an absolute construction attached to Chaos's court. S17-S19 -> L22-L24a: Night sits with Chaos on the stated throne, wears black, participates in his rule, and is eldest. Night's partner status precedes her age locally; partner avoids inventing equality of authority. Her shared enthronement is compressed into sits with him after the throne, not a new adjacent chair. S19-S23 -> L24b-L28: Orcus, Hades, Demogorgon's dreaded name, Rumor, Chance, Tumult, Confusion, Discord occur in source order. Only Demogorgon's name is asserted; no visible body is invented. Held in dread attaches to that name across L25-L26. All tangled together at L27 describes the preceding embroiled company; it does not make one allegory attack another. L28 keeps a thousand various mouths literally; the colon compresses the source's with, without adding speech, tongues, or mutual quarrels. Amplification inventory is exhausted by L01 (failed watch), L02 (physical removal), L05 (terrain resistance), L09 (footing relinquished in flight), L12 (intelligibility lost), L13 (dark made carrier), L14 (assault entering), L15 (fear as negated restraint), L19 (dammed boundary), and L20 (court). None adds a separate episode. Kenning and epithet ledger: No coined compound noun or compound epithet survives; the ordinary lexical compounds are recorded below as well. L05 "broken climbs": the source's rough steeps become obstacles that the staving battles must overcome; this is the only added descriptive terrain epithet. L14 "utmost onslaught": maximal violence presses against the receiving ear, preserving the source's loudest assault rather than ornamenting its mood. L17 "lowest abyss": literal source locator placing the possible inhabitant at the extreme opposite of the sought exit; its line honestly FAILs the stave-tension test. L19 "darkness, dammed": darkness is checked at its border with daylight; no physical dam is asserted. L21 "black pavilion": literal inherited description joining royal shelter to the surrounding dark waste; this is not offered as a successful arguing kenning, and its line is a declared FAIL. L23 "his partner in rule": inherited relational title, setting Night beside the ruler rather than among the standing attendants; not a coined kenning or a semantic PASS. L24 "the eldest of things": inherited superlative setting Night's age above that of the other things; its line remains a FAIL rather than pretending that eldest/Orcus argues. L19 "daylight's edge": the source's light-facing coast turns extent into limitation, binding DAY-light to DAMMED darkness. L04 "through the wild—so the fiend": fiend is the source's designation of Satan, set against the path he fights to obtain; the line FAILs because fiend/fights alone is ordinary agency. L09 "footholds": an ordinary lexical compound for the support relinquished in flight, opposed to staving feet freed from it. L10 "an uproar": uproar is an ordinary lexical compound, not a coined kenning; its onset is UP, while e-RUPTS has r and cannot supply the claimed vowel verb-stave. L19 "daylight": daylight is an ordinary lexical compound naming the realm that bounds darkness, not a decorative mood-word. Descriptive predicates such as stunning and entangled preserve events or states already in the source; they are not disguised coined epithets. Ledger entries distinguish inherited literal descriptors from kennings: the former are retained for fidelity with honest failures; no unjustifiable coined kenning is excused. Hybrid re-audit and stop: 8 lines PASS outright; L01 passes the tension test but FAILs meter, and L02 passes the tension test but FAILs the verb-target requirement; 18 further lines retain stated formal or semantic FAILs. Stop after this revision: each remaining failure has a line-local fidelity justification, and further action assigned to the cataloged entities would change the translation. The audit is the limit claim: this is a documented compromise, not a claim that every line meets the cardinal rule. -->

Retained for the named thieves and their theft; OFF is subordinate. --> \
> on wings over hills || and hollows of marsh, <!-- L03; 2+2: WINGS HILLS || HOLL-ows MARSH [h: xa|ax] {opposition: hills ↔ hollows} FAIL-verb: retained for the flying griffin's high and low terrain; no h-staving verb. --> \
> through the wild—so the fiend || fights on, eager. <!-- L04; 2+2: WILD FIEND || FIGHTS EA-ger [f: xa|ax] {none} FAIL-tension: fiend/fights is ordinary agency; retained to turn the simile and state eagerness; ON is subordinate. --> \
>
> He battles bogs || and broken climbs, <!-- L05; 2+2: BATT-les BOGS || BROK-en CLIMBS [b: aa|ax] {violence: battles ↔ bogs / broken climbs} PASS: traversed terrain becomes resistance; GUARDRAIL: battles is figurative struggle, not an added combat. --> \
> through strait and rough, || through the dense and the thin; <!-- L06; 2+2: STRAIT ROUGH || DENSE THIN [—: xx|xx] {none} FAIL-stave: retained to preserve narrow passage, roughness, and opposed densities; these are media, not characters. --> \
> with head and hands, || with wings and feet, <!-- L07; 2+2: HEAD HANDS || WINGS FEET [—: xx|xx] {none} FAIL-stave: retained to preserve all four body parts in source order without invented limb-actions. --> \
> he swims or sinks, || or wades or creeps, <!-- L08; 2+2: SWIMS SINKS || WADES CREEPS [—: xx|xx] {none across caesura} FAIL-stave: swims/sinks oppose each other only inside the first half; retained for the ordered catalog. --> \
> or flies, with feet || freed from their footholds. <!-- L09; 2+2: FLIES FEET || FREED FOOT-holds [f: aa|aa] {privation: feet / freed ↔ footholds} PASS; GUARDRAIL: all four lifts stave; flight relinquishes footing, without an added stumble or injury. -->

> all sounds and voices || stunning, entangled. <!-- L11; 2+2: SOUNDS VOIC-es || STUN-ning en-TANG-led [—: xx|xx] {none} FAIL-stave: s does not match st; retained for the explicit stunning sounds and confused voices; ALL is subordinate. --> \
> Clamor crushes || the clarity of speech; <!-- L12; 2+2: CLAM-or CRUSH-es || CLAR-i-ty SPEECH [k: aa|ax] {violence: clamor / crushes ↔ clarity} PASS: aggregate noise destroys intelligibility; cl/cr share k under the declared cluster rule. --> \

> its utmost onslaught || entering his ear. <!-- L14; 2+2: UT-most ON-slaught || EN-ter-ing EAR [V: aa|aa] {violence: onslaught / entering ↔ ear} PASS; GUARDRAIL: the final lift also staves; utmost preserves maximal force without alleging lasting injury. -->
>

> to face whatever || spirit or power <!-- L16; 2+2: FACE what-EV-er || SPIR-it POW-er [—: xx|xx] {none} FAIL-stave: retained for the indefinite power or spirit; the two alternatives reverse locally to sustain the line. --> \

> wide over the deep. || Night sits with him, <!-- L22; 2+2: WIDE DEEP || NIGHT SITS [—: xx|xx] {none} FAIL-stave: retained for breadth, the deep, and shared enthronement; Night sits with the holder of the preceding throne, not at a separate seat. --> \
> in robes of black, || his partner in rule, <!-- L23; 2+2: ROBES BLACK || PART-ner RULE [r: ax|xa] {none} FAIL-head-stave and tension: costume/rule is not an argument; retained for black clothing and participation in his reign. --> \
> the eldest of things. || And Orcus and Hades <!-- L24; 2+2: ELD-est THINGS || OR-cus HA-des [V: ax|ax] {none} FAIL-verb and tension: age and the attendants are separate facts; retained to finish Night's identity and begin the named list. -->
>

> is held in dread; || then Rumor and Chance, <!-- L26; 2+2: HELD DREAD || RU-mor CHANCE [—: xx|xx] {none} FAIL-stave: retained for dread attaching to Demogorgon's name, followed by Rumor and Chance. --> \
> then Tumult, Confusion, || all tangled together; <!-- L27; 2+2: TU-mult con-FU-sion || TANG-led to-GETH-er [t: ax|ax] {none} FAIL-tension: Tumult/tangled merely agree; retained for the ordered names and their collective embroilment; ALL is subordinate. --> \
> and Discord: a thousand || various mouths. <!-- L28; 2+2: DIS-cord THOU-sand || VAR-i-ous MOUTHS [—: xx|xx] {none} FAIL-stave: retained for Discord's exact number and bodily variety without inventing speech or self-conflict. -->
> </div>
>
> ...Fable’s is substantially better than mine. Opus’s is better poetry too. Mine preserves more details and audits itself more skeptically, but I let that become a substitute for writing a good poem...I would use Fable as the base, borrow Opus’s compression and handling of the movement catalog, and use mine principally as a fidelity checklist. A few of my lines are worth retaining as candidates—especially [“dark drives || the din through its hollows”]{.poem}—but the poem needs substantially more reconstruction than its annotations suggest.

## Claude-5.5-Opus {#claude-55-opus}

[Claude-5.5-Opus](https://www.anthropic.com/claude-opus-5-5) Max (2026-09-25):

> <div class="poem">
> As the hoardless griffin || hunts through the wilderness, \
> hawking over hill || and hollow of fen, \
> straining after the Arimaspians, || who by stealth had taken \
> the gold he guarded: || so greedily the fiend \
> over bog and bluff || breasts his way onward, \
> racked on the rough, || wrenched through the strait, \
> driving through the dense, || dropped by the rare, \
> with head and with hands, || with heel and with wing; \
> he swims and he sinks, || he slithers, wades, flies. \
>
> At length the waste || wakes into uproar, \
> numbing noise || and a knot of voices; \
> it hammers his head, || hurled through the dark, \
> loud past all bearing. || He bears that way, \
> undaunted by dread, || diving to meet \
> whatever force || or phantom, in the din, \
> broods at the bottom || of the bottomless gulf, \
> to ask of that dark one || where the dark fails, \
> its dwindling coast, || with daylight nearest.
>
> Then Chaos came clear, || crowned on his throne, \
> his pavilion vaulting || the void in darkness; \
> noonless Night || sat next to him in state, \
> black-robed, his bride, || born before all; \
> Orcus and Hades, || the hidden, stood by them, \
> and the dreaded name, || Demogorgon; \
> then Rumor, and Chance || that chooses nothing, \
> and Tumult and Confusion, || fused in one clinch, \
> and Discord, many-mouthed, || a mismatched thousand.
> </div>
